Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The reactors operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 295. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 145 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 295 + O(1/n^145). Summing over all modes and applying the reactors convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
